  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}

  DiamondIntegral.Section7.BlowupCongruence.MuIsp x y →
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.BlowupCongruence.MuIsp x' y' →
      DiamondIntegral.Section7.BlowupCongruence.MuIsp (x + x') (y + y') :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {k}
    {x y x' y'} h h' =>
  Exists.casesOn h fun c h =>
    Exists.casesOn h fun m h =>
      Exists.casesOn h fun n h =>
        And.casesOn h fun hx hy =>
          Exists.casesOn h' fun c' h =>
            Exists.casesOn h fun m' h =>
              Exists.casesOn h fun n' h =>
                And.casesOn h fun hx' hy' =>
                  Exists.intro (c + c')
                    (Exists.intro (m + m')
                      (Exists.intro (n + n')
                        ⟨DiamondIntegral.Section7.BlowupCongruence.steps_add hx hx',
                          DiamondIntegral.Section7.BlowupCongruence.steps_add hy hy'⟩))

theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.graphOn_inter : ∀ {M : Type} {m n : ℕ} (I J : Set (Fin m → M))

  DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn (I ∩ J) F =

@DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.graph_inter_definable : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]

      SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B

theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.graph_inter_definable : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {m n : ℕ} (I J : Set (Fin m → M))
  (F : (Fin m → M) → Fin n → M),
  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn I F) →
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
        (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) J →
      SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
        (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn (I ∩ J) F) :=
fun {M} [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M]
    [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {m n} I J F hG hJ =>
  have h :=
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase.inter hG
      (SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase.preimage_real hJ
        (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex n m));
  Eq.mpr
    (eq_of_heq
      ((fun M sM hM B n sn A A' e'_7 =>
          Eq.casesOn (motive := fun a x =>
            A' = a →
              e'_7 ≍ x →
                SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A ≍
                  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A')
            e'_7
            (fun h =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun A' =>
                ∀ (e_7 : A = A'),
                  e_7 ≍ Eq.refl A →
                    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A ≍
                      SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A')
                (fun e_7 h => HEq.refl (SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A))
                (Eq.symm h) e'_7)
            (Eq.refl A') (HEq.refl e'_7))
        M inst hM B (n + m) (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn (I ∩ J) F)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn I F ∩
          {z |
            z ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex n m ∈ J ∧
              z ∈
                SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.sortedUniverse fun x =>
                  SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv})
        (Set.ext fun w =>
          {
            mp := fun hw =>
              ⟨⟨hw.left.left, hw.right⟩,
                ⟨hw.left.right,
                  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase.proper hG w
                    ⟨hw.left.left, hw.right⟩⟩⟩,
            mpr := fun hw => ⟨⟨hw.left.left, hw.right.left⟩, hw.left.right⟩ })))
    h

@DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.graph_image_definable : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {m n : ℕ} (I : Set (Fin m → M))
  (F : (Fin m → M) → Fin n → M),
  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn I F) →
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) (F '' I)
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.graph_image_definable : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {m n : ℕ} (I : Set (Fin m → M))
  (F : (Fin m → M) → Fin n → M),
  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn I F) →
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) (F '' I) :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B}
    {m n} I F hG =>
  have h :=
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase.image_real hG
      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex n m);
  have he :=
    Set.ext fun y =>
      {
        mp := fun a =>
          Exists.casesOn a fun x h =>
            And.casesOn h fun hx right =>
              right ▸
                have hh := funext (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join_head n m x (F x));
                have ht := funext (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join_tail n m x (F x));
                Exists.intro (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join n m x (F x))
                  ⟨⟨Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a ∈ I) hh)) hx,
                      Eq.mpr
                        (id
                          (congrArg
                            (fun _a =>
                              F _a =
                                DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join n m x (F x) ∘
                                  DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex n m)
                            hh))
                        (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => F x = _a) ht)) (Eq.refl (F x)))⟩,
                    ht⟩,
        mpr := fun a =>
          Exists.casesOn a fun w h =>
            And.casesOn h fun hw right =>
              right ▸ Exists.intro (w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex n m) ⟨hw.left, hw.right⟩ };
  Eq.mpr
    (id
      (congrArg
        (fun _a =>
          SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
            (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) _a)
        he))
    h

 Quot.sound]

  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] →
    [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] →
      {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} →
        {k n m : ℕ} →
          (X : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartDisjointUnion.Data B n k) →
            (Y : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartDisjointUnion.Data B m k) →
              (I : Set (Fin n → M)) →
                (hI :
                    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
                      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) I) →
                  (J : Set (Fin m → M)) →
                    (hJ :
                        SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
                          (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) J) →
                      DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Iso
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.inside X I hI).toChart
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.inside Y J hJ).toChart →
                        DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Iso
                            (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.outside X I hI).toChart
                            (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.outside Y J hJ).toChart →
                          DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Iso X.toChart Y.toChart
def DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.glue : {M : Type} →
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] →
    [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] →
      {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} →
        {k n m : ℕ} →
          (X : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartDisjointUnion.Data B n k) →
            (Y : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartDisjointUnion.Data B m k) →
              (I : Set (Fin n → M)) →
                (hI :
                    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
                      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) I) →
                  (J : Set (Fin m → M)) →
                    (hJ :
                        SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
                          (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) J) →
                      DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Iso
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.inside X I hI).toChart
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.inside Y J hJ).toChart →
                        DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Iso
                            (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.outside X I hI).toChart
                            (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.outside Y J hJ).toChart →
                          DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Iso X.toChart Y.toChart :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B}
    {k n m} X Y I hI J hJ F G =>
  DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredIsomorphisms.trans
    (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredIsomorphisms.symm
      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.partitionIso X I hI))
    (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredIsomorphisms.trans
      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVDisjointUnionIsomorphisms.unionIso
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.outside X I hI)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.inside X I hI)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.outside Y J hJ)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.inside Y J hJ) ⋯ ⋯ G F)
      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.partitionIso Y J hJ))

                  (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.R a b).domain ⋯).toChart
              a :=

@DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image_compl_piece : ∀ {M : Type} {n m : ℕ} {f : (Fin n → M) → Fin m → M}
  {A P Q : Set (Fin n → M)} {C : Set (Fin m → M)},
  Set.BijOn f A C → A = P ∪ Q → Disjoint P Q → C \ f '' (A ∩ Q) = f '' (A ∩ P)
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image_compl_piece : ∀ {M : Type} {n m : ℕ} {f : (Fin n → M) → Fin m → M}
  {A P Q : Set (Fin n → M)} {C : Set (Fin m → M)},
  Set.BijOn f A C → A = P ∪ Q → Disjoint P Q → C \ f '' (A ∩ Q) = f '' (A ∩ P) :=
fun {M} {n m} {f} {A P Q} {C} hf hA hPQ =>
  Set.ext fun y =>
    {
      mp := fun a =>
        And.casesOn a fun hy hnot =>
          Exists.casesOn (hf.right.right hy) fun x h =>
            And.casesOn h fun hx right =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun y => y ∈ C → y ∉ f '' (A ∩ Q) → y ∈ f '' (A ∩ P))
                (fun hy hnot =>
                  have hx' := hA ▸ hx;
                  Or.casesOn hx' (fun hp => Exists.intro x ⟨⟨hx, hp⟩, rfl⟩) fun hq =>
                    absurd (Exists.intro x ⟨⟨hx, hq⟩, rfl⟩) hnot)
                right hy hnot,
      mpr := fun a =>
        Exists.casesOn a fun x h =>
          And.casesOn h fun left right =>
            And.casesOn left fun hx hp =>
              right ▸
                ⟨hf.left hx, fun a =>
                  Exists.casesOn a fun x' h =>
                    And.casesOn h fun left he =>
                      And.casesOn left fun hx' hq =>
                        have hxx := hf.right.left hx' hx he;
                        Eq.ndrec (motive := fun x => x ∈ A → x ∈ P → f x' = f x → False)
                          (fun hx hp he => Set.disjoint_left.mp hPQ hp hq) hxx hx hp he⟩ }
'DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image_compl_piece' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image_compl_domain : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}
  {a b : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Chart B k} (F : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Iso a b)
  (J : Set (Fin a.arity → M)),
  b.domain \ DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image F J =
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image F (a.domain \ J) :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {k}
    {a b} F J =>
  have h :=
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image_compl_piece (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.isoMap_bijOn F)
      (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => a.domain = _a) (Set.sdiff_union_inter a.domain J))) (Eq.refl a.domain))
      Set.disjoint_sdiff_inter;

    (Eq.mp
      (congrArg
        (fun _a =>
          b.domain \ DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.isoMap F '' (_a ∩ J) =
            DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.isoMap F '' (a.domain ∩ (a.domain \ J)))

          (fun _a =>
            b.domain \ DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.isoMap F '' _a =
              DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.isoMap F '' (a.domain ∩ (a.domain \ J)))

        h))

@DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.refine_iso : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}
  (X Y : DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.Obj B k)
  (Φ : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Iso X.toChart Y.toChart) (I : Set (Fin X.arity → M))
  (hI :
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) I)
  (J : Set (Fin Y.arity → M))
  (hJ :
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) J),
  ∃ e f g h,
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece X I hI) = e + f ∧
      DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cpiece X I hI) = g + h ∧
        DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece Y J hJ) = e + g ∧
          DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cpiece Y J hJ) = f + h
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.refine_iso : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}
  (X Y : DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.Obj B k)
  (Φ : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Iso X.toChart Y.toChart) (I : Set (Fin X.arity → M))
  (hI :
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) I)
  (J : Set (Fin Y.arity → M))
  (hJ :
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) J),
  ∃ e f g h,
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece X I hI) = e + f ∧
      DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cpiece X I hI) = g + h ∧
        DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece Y J hJ) = e + g ∧
          DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cpiece Y J hJ) = f + h :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {k}
    X Y Φ I hI J hJ =>
  let Ψ := DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredIsomorphisms.symm Φ;
  let K := DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image Ψ J;
  have hK := DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image_definable Ψ J hJ;
  Exists.intro
    (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece X I hI) K hK))
    (Exists.intro
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
        (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cpiece (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece X I hI) K hK))
      (Exists.intro
        (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cpiece X I hI) K hK))
        (Exists.intro
          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
            (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cpiece (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cpiece X I hI) K
              hK))
          ⟨DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_partition (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece X I hI)
              K hK,
            ⟨DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_partition
                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cpiece X I hI) K hK,
              ⟨have h1 :=
                  have h := DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_piece_image Ψ J hJ;
                  h;
                Eq.mpr
                  (id
                    (congrArg
                      (fun _a =>
                        _a =
                          DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece
                                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece X I hI) K hK) +
                            DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece
                                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cpiece X I hI) K hK))
                      h1))
                  (Eq.mpr
                    (id
                      (congrArg
                        (fun _a =>
                          _a =
                            DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece
                                  (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece X I hI) K hK) +
                              DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece
                                  (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cpiece X I hI) K hK))
                        (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_partition
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece X K hK) I hI)))
                    ((fun {α β γ} [HAdd α β γ] a a_1 e_a =>
                        Eq.rec (motive := fun a_2 e_a => ∀ (a_3 a_4 : β), a_3 = a_4 → a + a_3 = a_2 + a_4)
                          (fun a_2 a_3 e_a => e_a ▸ Eq.refl (a + a_2)) e_a)
                      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                        (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece X K hK) I hI))
                      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                        (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece X I hI) K hK))
                      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_eq_of_iso
                        (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.sameIso

  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}
  (p q r s : DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.Cls B k),
  p + q = r + s → ∃ e f g h', p = e + f ∧ q = g + h' ∧ r = e + g ∧ s = f + h' :=

    p q r s h =>
  Exists.casesOn (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_surjective p) fun P h_1 =>
    Eq.ndrec (motive := fun p => p + q = r + s → ∃ e f g h', p = e + f ∧ q = g + h' ∧ r = e + g ∧ s = f + h')
      (fun h =>
        Exists.casesOn (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_surjective q) fun Q h_2 =>
          Eq.ndrec (motive := fun q =>
            DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls P + q = r + s →
              ∃ e f g h', DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls P = e + f ∧ q = g + h' ∧ r = e + g ∧ s = f + h')
            (fun h =>
              Exists.casesOn (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_surjective r) fun R' h_3 =>
                Eq.ndrec (motive := fun r =>
                  DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls P + DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls Q =
                      r + s →
                    ∃ e f g h',
                      DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls P = e + f ∧
                        DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls Q = g + h' ∧ r = e + g ∧ s = f + h')
                  (fun h =>
                    Exists.casesOn (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_surjective s) fun S h_4 =>
                      Eq.ndrec (motive := fun s =>
                        DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls P + DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls Q =
                            DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls R' + s →
                          ∃ e f g h',
                            DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls P = e + f ∧
                              DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls Q = g + h' ∧
                                DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls R' = e + g ∧ s = f + h')
                        (fun h =>
                          Nonempty.casesOn
                            (Eq.mp
                              (congrArg (fun _a => _a) (propext DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_eq_iff))

                                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.L R'.toChart S.toChart).domain
                                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.L R'.toChart S.toChart).domain_definable)

theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.eq_zero_of_add_eq_zero : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}
  (x y : DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.Cls B k), x + y = 0 → x = 0 :=

    x y h =>
  Exists.casesOn (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_surjective x) fun a h_1 =>
    Eq.ndrec (motive := fun x => x + y = 0 → x = 0)
      (fun h =>
        Exists.casesOn (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_surjective y) fun b h_2 =>
          Eq.ndrec (motive := fun y =>
            DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls a + y = 0 → DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls a = 0)
            (fun h =>
              Nonempty.casesOn
                (Eq.mp (congrArg (fun _a => _a) (propext DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_eq_iff))
                  (Eq.mp
                    (congrArg
                      (fun _a =>
                        DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls

                          _a)
                      (Eq.symm DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_zero))
                    (Eq.mp
                      (congrArg (fun _a => _a = 0) (Eq.symm (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_coprod a b)))
                      h)))
                fun Φ =>
                DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_empty a
                  (Set.eq_empty_iff_forall_notMem.mpr fun u hu =>
                    let v :=
                      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredIsomorphisms.symm
                            (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVTaggedComponentIsomorphisms.leftIso a.toChart b.toChart)).equiv
                        ⟨u, hu⟩;

                    (Φ.equiv ⟨↑v, hv⟩).property))
            h_2 h)
      h_1 h

@DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_eq_zero_iff : ∀ {M : Type}

  DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls a = 0 ↔ a.domain = ∅
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_eq_zero_iff : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}
  (a : DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.Obj B k),
  DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls a = 0 ↔ a.domain = ∅ :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {k}
    a =>
  {
    mp := fun h =>
      Nonempty.casesOn
        (Eq.mp (congrArg (fun _a => _a) (propext DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_eq_iff))
          (Eq.mp
            (congrArg (fun _a => DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls a = _a)

  (a : DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.Obj B k) (p q : DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.Cls B k),

    ∃ I,
      ∃ (hI :
        SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
          (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) I),

          DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cpiece a I hI) = q :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {k}
    a p q h =>
  Exists.casesOn (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_surjective p) fun P h_1 =>
    Eq.ndrec (motive := fun p =>
      DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls a = p + q →
        ∃ I,
          ∃ (hI :
            SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
              (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) I),
            DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece a I hI) = p ∧
              DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cpiece a I hI) = q)
      (fun h =>
        Exists.casesOn (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_surjective q) fun Q h_2 =>
          Eq.ndrec (motive := fun q =>
            DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls a = DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls P + q →
              ∃ I,
                ∃ (hI :
                  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
                    (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) I),
                  DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.piece a I hI) =
                      DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls P ∧
                    DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                        (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cpiece a I hI) =
                      q)
            (fun h =>
              Nonempty.casesOn
                (Eq.mp (congrArg (fun _a => _a) (propext DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_eq_iff))
                  (Eq.mp
                    (congrArg (fun _a => DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls a = _a)
                      (Eq.symm (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_coprod P Q)))
                    h))
                fun Φ =>
                let Ψ := DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredIsomorphisms.symm Φ;
                Exists.intro
                  (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image Ψ
                    (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.L P.toChart Q.toChart).domain)
                  (Exists.intro
                    (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image_definable Ψ
                      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.L P.toChart Q.toChart).domain
                      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.L P.toChart Q.toChart).domain_definable)

                        (id

                            (Eq.symm

                        (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_eq_of_iso

DiamondIntegral.Section7.Refinement.Refine2 : (α : Type u_1) → [AddCommMonoid α] → Prop
def DiamondIntegral.Section7.Refinement.Refine2.{u_1} : (α : Type u_1) → [AddCommMonoid α] → Prop :=
fun α [AddCommMonoid α] => ∀ (p q r s : α), p + q = r + s → ∃ e f g h, p = e + f ∧ q = g + h ∧ r = e + g ∧ s = f + h

DiamondIntegral.Section7.Refinement.ZeroSumFree : (α : Type u_1) → [AddCommMonoid α] → Prop
def DiamondIntegral.Section7.Refinement.ZeroSumFree.{u_1} : (α : Type u_1) → [AddCommMonoid α] → Prop :=
fun α [AddCommMonoid α] => ∀ (x y : α), x + y = 0 → x = 0
'DiamondIntegral.Section7.Refinement.ZeroSumFree' does not depend on any axioms

  DiamondIntegral.Section7.Refinement.ZeroSumFree α → ∀ {m : ℕ} (b : Fin m → α), ∑ j, b j = 0 → ∀ (j : Fin m), b j = 0

  [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] {m n : ℕ} (I J : Set (Fin m → M)) (F : (Fin m → M) → Fin n → M),
  DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn (I \ J) F =
    DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn I F ∩
      {w | w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex n m ∉ J}
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.graphOn_diff : ∀ {M : Type}
  [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] {m n : ℕ} (I J : Set (Fin m → M)) (F : (Fin m → M) → Fin n → M),
  DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn (I \ J) F =
    DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn I F ∩
      {w | w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex n m ∉ J} :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] {m n} I J F =>
  Set.ext fun w =>
    { mp := fun h => ⟨⟨h.left.left, h.right⟩, h.left.right⟩, mpr := fun h => ⟨⟨h.left.left, h.right⟩, h.left.right⟩ }

@DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.graph_diff_definable : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {m n : ℕ} (I J : Set (Fin m → M))
  (F : (Fin m → M) → Fin n → M),
  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn I F) →
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
        (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) J →
      SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
        (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn (I \ J) F)
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.graph_diff_definable : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {m n : ℕ} (I J : Set (Fin m → M))
  (F : (Fin m → M) → Fin n → M),
  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn I F) →
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
        (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) J →
      SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
        (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn (I \ J) F) :=
fun {M} [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M]
    [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {m n} I J F hG hJ =>
  have hP :=
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase.preimage_real hJ
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)
      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex n m);
  have h :=
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase.inter hG
      (SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase.compl hP);
  Eq.mpr
    (eq_of_heq
      ((fun M sM hM B n sn A A' e'_7 =>
          Eq.casesOn (motive := fun a x =>
            A' = a →
              e'_7 ≍ x →
                SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A ≍
                  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A')
            e'_7
            (fun h =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun A' =>
                ∀ (e_7 : A = A'),
                  e_7 ≍ Eq.refl A →
                    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A ≍
                      SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A')
                (fun e_7 h => HEq.refl (SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A))
                (Eq.symm h) e'_7)
            (Eq.refl A') (HEq.refl e'_7))
        M inst hM B (n + m) (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn (I \ J) F)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn I F ∩
          ((SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.sortedUniverse fun x =>
              SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) \
            {z |
              z ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex n m ∈ J ∧
                z ∈
                  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.sortedUniverse fun x =>
                    SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv}))
        (Set.ext fun w =>
          {
            mp := fun hw =>
              have hg := ⟨hw.left.left, hw.right⟩;
              ⟨hg,
                ⟨SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase.proper hG w hg, fun hp =>
                  hw.left.right hp.left⟩⟩,
            mpr := fun hw => ⟨⟨hw.left.left, fun hj => hw.right.right ⟨hj, hw.right.left⟩⟩, hw.left.right⟩ })))
    h
'DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.graph_diff_definable' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]

  w ∈ DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.shellDomain r τ ↔

          SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (w (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.sIdx r)) ≤
            SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
              ((w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 (1 + r.arity))

    r τ w =>
  have h :=
    DiamondIntegral.Section4.VariableApertureRayCancellation.annularChart_join_iff
      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.graphChart r τ) (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.aIdx r)
      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.graph_aperture_nonzero r τ)
      (w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 (1 + r.arity))
      (w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 (1 + r.arity));

    (congrArg
      (fun _a =>
        _a ∈
            (DiamondIntegral.Section4.VariableApertureRayCancellation.annularChart
                (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.graphChart r τ)
                (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.aIdx r)).domain ↔

              (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.graphChart r τ).domain ∧

                (w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 (1 + r.arity)) ∧
              1 <
                  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                    ((w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 (1 + r.arity)) 0) ∧
                SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                    ((w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 (1 + r.arity)) 0) ≤
                  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                    ((w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 (1 + r.arity))
                      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.aIdx r)))
      (have this := DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join_reconstruct 1 (1 + r.arity) w;

@DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shellDomain_res : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}
  (r : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Chart B k) (τ : DiamondIntegral.Section4.AnnularBlowup.Aperture r)
  (I : Set (Fin r.arity → M))
  (hI :
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) I),
  DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.shellDomain (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.resChart r I hI)
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.apRes τ I hI) =
    DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.shellDomain r τ ∩ DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shellCut r I
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shellDomain_res : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}
  (r : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Chart B k) (τ : DiamondIntegral.Section4.AnnularBlowup.Aperture r)
  (I : Set (Fin r.arity → M))
  (hI :
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) I),
  DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.shellDomain (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.resChart r I hI)
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.apRes τ I hI) =
    DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.shellDomain r τ ∩ DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shellCut r I :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {k}
    r τ I hI =>
  Set.ext fun w =>
    Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg
          (fun _a =>
            w ∈
                DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.shellDomain
                  (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.resChart r I hI)
                  (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.apRes τ I hI) ↔
              _a)
          (propext
            (Set.mem_inter_iff w (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.shellDomain r τ)
              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shellCut r I)))))
      (Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg
            (fun _a =>
              _a ↔
                w ∈ DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.shellDomain r τ ∧
                  w ∈ DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shellCut r I)
            (propext
              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shell_iff
                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.resChart r I hI)
                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.apRes τ I hI) w))))
        (Eq.mpr
          (id
            (congrArg
              (fun _a =>
                w ∘
                        DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1
                          (1 + (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.resChart r I hI).arity) ∈
                      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.graphChart
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.resChart r I hI)
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.apRes τ I hI)).domain ∧
                    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Proper
                        (w ∘
                          DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1
                            (1 + (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.resChart r I hI).arity)) ∧
                      1 <
                          SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                            (w
                              (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.sIdx
                                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.resChart r I hI))) ∧
                        SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                            (w
                              (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.sIdx
                                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.resChart r I hI))) ≤
                          SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                            ((w ∘
                                DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1
                                  (1 + (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.resChart r I hI).arity))
                              (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.aIdx
                                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.resChart r I hI))) ↔
                  _a ∧ w ∈ DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shellCut r I)
              (propext (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shell_iff r τ w))))
          {
            mp := fun a =>
              And.casesOn a fun left right =>
                And.casesOn left fun left he =>
                  And.casesOn left fun hd hi =>
                    And.casesOn right fun hp right =>
                      And.casesOn right fun h1 h2 =>
                        have hw :=
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shell_iff r τ w).mpr ⟨⟨hd, he⟩, ⟨hp, ⟨h1, h2⟩⟩⟩;
                        ⟨⟨⟨hd, he⟩, ⟨hp, ⟨h1, h2⟩⟩⟩,
                          ⟨hi, DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shell_sorted r τ hw⟩⟩,
            mpr := fun a =>
              And.casesOn a fun left right =>
                And.casesOn left fun left right_1 =>
                  And.casesOn left fun hd he =>
                    And.casesOn right_1 fun hp right_2 =>
                      And.casesOn right_2 fun h1 h2 =>
                        And.casesOn right fun hi right => ⟨⟨⟨hd, hi⟩, he⟩, ⟨hp, ⟨h1, h2⟩⟩⟩ }))
'DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shellDomain_res' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
@DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shellDomain_cres : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}

theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shellDomain_cres : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}
  (r : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Chart B k) (τ : DiamondIntegral.Section4.AnnularBlowup.Aperture r)

      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) I),
  DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.shellDomain (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cresChart r I hI)
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.apCres τ I hI) =
    DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.shellDomain r τ \ DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shellCut r I :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {k}
    r τ I hI =>
  Set.ext fun w =>
    Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg
          (fun _a =>
            w ∈
                DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.shellDomain
                  (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cresChart r I hI)
                  (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.apCres τ I hI) ↔
              _a)
          (propext (Set.mem_sdiff w))))
      (Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg
            (fun _a =>
              _a ↔
                w ∈ DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.shellDomain r τ ∧
                  w ∉ DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shellCut r I)
            (propext
              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shell_iff
                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cresChart r I hI)
                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.apCres τ I hI) w))))
        (Eq.mpr
          (id
            (congrArg
              (fun _a =>
                w ∘
                        DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1
                          (1 + (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cresChart r I hI).arity) ∈
                      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.graphChart
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cresChart r I hI)
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.apCres τ I hI)).domain ∧
                    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Proper
                        (w ∘
                          DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1
                            (1 + (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cresChart r I hI).arity)) ∧
                      1 <
                          SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                            (w
                              (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.sIdx
                                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cresChart r I hI))) ∧
                        SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                            (w
                              (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.sIdx
                                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cresChart r I hI))) ≤
                          SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                            ((w ∘
                                DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1
                                  (1 + (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cresChart r I hI).arity))
                              (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.aIdx
                                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cresChart r I hI))) ↔
                  _a ∧ w ∉ DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shellCut r I)
              (propext (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shell_iff r τ w))))
          {
            mp := fun a =>
              And.casesOn a fun left right =>
                And.casesOn left fun left he =>
                  And.casesOn left fun hd hi =>
                    And.casesOn right fun hp right =>
                      And.casesOn right fun h1 h2 => ⟨⟨⟨hd, he⟩, ⟨hp, ⟨h1, h2⟩⟩⟩, fun hc => hi hc.left⟩,
            mpr := fun a =>
              And.casesOn a fun left hc =>
                And.casesOn left fun left right =>
                  And.casesOn left fun hd he =>
                    And.casesOn right fun hp right =>
                      And.casesOn right fun h1 h2 =>
                        have hw :=
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shell_iff r τ w).mpr ⟨⟨hd, he⟩, ⟨hp, ⟨h1, h2⟩⟩⟩;
                        ⟨⟨⟨hd, fun hi => hc ⟨hi, DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.shell_sorted r τ hw⟩⟩, he⟩,
                          ⟨hp, ⟨h1, h2⟩⟩⟩ }))

@DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cls_annular_eq_zero : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]

  DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.source = 0 →

theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cls_annular_eq_zero : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}
  (D : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RelativeAnnularGenerators.AnnularDatum B k),
  DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.source = 0 →
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.annularObject = 0 :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {k}
    D h =>
  have hD := (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_eq_zero_iff D.source).mp h;
  have hs :=
    (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_eq_zero_iff
          (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupRelation.shellBounded D.chart D.coordinate D.finite D.aperture D.bounded)).mpr
      (Set.eq_empty_iff_forall_notMem.mpr fun w hw =>
        have hx := (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.shell_points D.chart D.aperture hw).left;
        Eq.mp
          (congrArg
            (fun _a =>
              (w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.xIdx D.chart,
                    w (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.sIdx D.chart)).1 ∈
                _a)
            hD)
          hx);
  have he :=
    (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_eq_zero_iff
          (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupRelation.endpointBounded D.chart D.coordinate D.finite D.aperture
            D.bounded)).mpr
      (Set.eq_empty_iff_forall_notMem.mpr fun w hw =>
        have hx := hw.left;
        Eq.mp (congrArg (fun _a => w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 D.chart.arity ∈ _a) hD)
          hx);
  Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = 0) (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cls_annular D)))
    (Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg
          (fun _a =>
            _a +
                DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                  (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupRelation.endpointBounded D.chart D.coordinate D.finite D.aperture
                    D.bounded) =
              0)
          hs))
      (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => 0 + _a = 0) he))
        (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = 0) (add_zero 0))) (Eq.refl 0))))

@DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datum_split : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}
  (D : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RelativeAnnularGenerators.AnnularDatum B k)
  (p q : DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.Cls B (1 + k)),
  DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.source = p + q →
    ∃ D₁ D₂,
      DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D₁.source = p ∧
        DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D₂.source = q ∧
          DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.annularObject =
            DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D₁.annularObject +
              DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D₂.annularObject

  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}
  (D : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RelativeAnnularGenerators.AnnularDatum B k)
  (p q : DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.Cls B (1 + k)),
  DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.source = p + q →
    ∃ D₁ D₂,
      DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D₁.source = p ∧
        DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D₂.source = q ∧
          DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.annularObject =
            DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D₁.annularObject +
              DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D₂.annularObject :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {k}
    D p q h =>
  Exists.casesOn (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.exists_split D.source p q h) fun I h =>
    Exists.casesOn h fun hI h =>
      And.casesOn h fun h1 h2 =>
        Exists.intro (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumRes D I hI)
          (Exists.intro (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumCres D I hI)
            ⟨h1, ⟨h2, DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cls_annular_split D I hI⟩⟩)

@DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datum_split_fin : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}

  (p : Fin m → DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.Cls B (1 + k)),
  DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.source = ∑ j, p j →
    ∃ E,
      (∀ (j : Fin m), DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E j).source = p j) ∧
        ∑ j, DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E j).annularObject =
          DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.annularObject
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datum_split_fin : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ}
  (D : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RelativeAnnularGenerators.AnnularDatum B k) (m : ℕ)
  (p : Fin m → DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.Cls B (1 + k)),
  DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.source = ∑ j, p j →
    ∃ E,
      (∀ (j : Fin m), DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E j).source = p j) ∧
        ∑ j, DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E j).annularObject =
          DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.annularObject :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {k}
    D m =>
  Nat.recAux (motive := fun m =>
    ∀ (D : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RelativeAnnularGenerators.AnnularDatum B k)
      (p : Fin m → DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.Cls B (1 + k)),
      DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.source = ∑ j, p j →
        ∃ E,
          (∀ (j : Fin m), DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E j).source = p j) ∧
            ∑ j, DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E j).annularObject =
              DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.annularObject)
    (fun D p h =>
      Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg Exists
            (funext fun E =>
              congrArg (And (∀ (j : Fin 0), DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E j).source = p j))
                (congrFun'
                  (congrArg Eq
                    (Finset.sum_congr Finset.univ_eq_empty fun x a =>
                      Eq.refl (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E x).annularObject)))
                  (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.annularObject)))))
        (Exists.intro Fin.elim0
          ⟨fun j => Fin.elim0 j,
            Eq.symm
              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cls_annular_eq_zero D
                (Eq.mp
                  (congrArg (Eq (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.source))
                    (Finset.sum_congr Finset.univ_eq_empty fun x a => Eq.refl (p x)))
                  h))⟩))
    (fun m ih D p h =>
      Exists.casesOn
        (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datum_split D (p 0) (∑ i, p i.succ)
          (Eq.mp (congrArg (fun _a => DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.source = _a) (Fin.sum_univ_succ p))
            h))
        fun D₁ h =>
        Exists.casesOn h fun D₂ h =>
          And.casesOn h fun h1 right =>
            And.casesOn right fun h2 h3 =>
              Exists.casesOn (ih D₂ (fun j => p j.succ) h2) fun E h =>
                And.casesOn h fun hE hs =>
                  Exists.intro (Fin.cons D₁ E)
                    ⟨fun j =>
                      Fin.cases
                        (Eq.mpr
                          (id
                            (congrFun'
                              (congrArg Eq
                                (congrArg DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                                  (congrArg DiamondIntegral.Section4.RelativeAnnularGenerators.AnnularDatum.source
                                    (Fin.cons_zero D₁ E))))
                              (p 0)))
                          h1)
                        (fun j =>
                          Eq.mpr
                            (id
                              (congrFun'
                                (congrArg Eq
                                  (congrArg DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                                    (congrArg DiamondIntegral.Section4.RelativeAnnularGenerators.AnnularDatum.source
                                      (Fin.cons_succ D₁ E j))))
                                (p j.succ)))
                            (hE j))
                        j,
                      Eq.mpr
                        (id
                          (congrArg (fun _a => _a = DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.annularObject)
                            (Fin.sum_univ_succ fun j =>
                              DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (Fin.cons D₁ E j).annularObject)))
                        (Eq.mpr
                          (id
                            (congrFun'
                              (congrArg Eq
                                (congr
                                  (congrArg HAdd.hAdd
                                    (congrArg DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                                      (congrArg
                                        DiamondIntegral.Section4.RelativeAnnularGenerators.AnnularDatum.annularObject
                                        (Fin.cons_zero D₁ E))))
                                  (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
                                    congrArg DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                                      (congrArg
                                        DiamondIntegral.Section4.RelativeAnnularGenerators.AnnularDatum.annularObject
                                        (Fin.cons_succ D₁ E x)))))
                              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.annularObject)))
                          (Eq.mpr
                            (id

                                (fun _a =>
                                  DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D₁.annularObject + _a =
                                    DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D.annularObject)

                                    DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D₁.annularObject +
                                        DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D₂.annularObject =
                                      _a)

                                  DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls D₂.annularObject)))))⟩)

fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {k}
    {x y x' y'} h h' =>
  Exists.casesOn h fun n h =>
    Exists.casesOn h fun D h =>
      Exists.casesOn h fun z h =>
        And.casesOn h fun left right =>
          Eq.symm left ▸
            Eq.symm right ▸
              Exists.casesOn h' fun n' h =>
                Exists.casesOn h fun D' h =>
                  Exists.casesOn h fun z' h =>
                    And.casesOn h fun left right =>
                      Eq.symm left ▸
                        Eq.symm right ▸
                          Exists.intro (n + n')
                            (Exists.intro (Fin.append D D')
                              (Exists.intro (z + z')
                                ⟨Eq.mpr
                                    (id
                                      (congrArg
                                        (fun _a =>
                                          z + ∑ i, DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (D i).source +
                                              (z' + ∑ i, DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (D' i).source) =
                                            z + z' + _a)
                                        (Fin.sum_univ_add fun i =>
                                          DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (Fin.append D D' i).source)))
                                    (Eq.mpr
                                      (id
                                        (congrArg
                                          (Eq
                                            (z + ∑ i, DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (D i).source +
                                              (z' + ∑ i, DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (D' i).source)))
                                          (congrArg (HAdd.hAdd (z + z'))
                                            (congr
                                              (congrArg HAdd.hAdd
                                                (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
                                                  congrArg DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                                                    (congrArg
                                                      DiamondIntegral.Section4.RelativeAnnularGenerators.AnnularDatum.source
                                                      (Fin.append_left D D' x))))
                                              (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
                                                congrArg DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                                                  (congrArg
                                                    DiamondIntegral.Section4.RelativeAnnularGenerators.AnnularDatum.source
                                                    (Fin.append_right D D' x)))))))
                                      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.step_add._abel_1_1 n D z n' D' z')),
                                  Eq.mpr
                                    (id
                                      (congrArg
                                        (fun _a =>
                                          z + ∑ i, DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (D i).annularObject +
                                              (z' +
                                                ∑ i, DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (D' i).annularObject) =
                                            z + z' + _a)
                                        (Fin.sum_univ_add fun i =>
                                          DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                                            (Fin.append D D' i).annularObject)))
                                    (Eq.mpr
                                      (id
                                        (congrArg
                                          (Eq

                                              (z' +
                                                ∑ i, DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (D' i).annularObject)))

          ∃ w,
            DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.Step y w ∧ DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.Step y' w
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.step_diamond : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ},
  DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.ElementaryDiamond B k →
    ∀ {x y y' : DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.Cls B (1 + k)},
      DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.Step x y →
        DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.Step x y' →
          ∃ w,
            DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.Step y w ∧
              DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.Step y' w :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {k}
    hE {x y y'} h₁ h₂ =>
  Exists.casesOn h₁ fun n h =>
    Exists.casesOn h fun D h =>
      Exists.casesOn h fun z h =>
        And.casesOn h fun hx hy =>
          Exists.casesOn h₂ fun m h =>
            Exists.casesOn h fun E h =>
              Exists.casesOn h fun z' h =>
                And.casesOn h fun hx' hy' =>
                  let a := Fin.cons z fun i => DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (D i).source;
                  let b := Fin.cons z' fun j => DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E j).source;
                  have hab :=
                    Eq.mpr
                      (id
                        (congr
                          (congrArg Eq
                            (Eq.trans
                              (Eq.trans
                                (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
                                  Eq.refl
                                    (Fin.cons z (fun i => DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (D i).source) x))
                                (Fin.sum_univ_succ
                                  (Fin.cons z fun i => DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (D i).source)))
                              (congr
                                (congrArg HAdd.hAdd
                                  (Fin.cons_zero z fun i => DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (D i).source))
                                (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
                                  Fin.cons_succ z (fun i => DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (D i).source)
                                    x))))
                          (Eq.trans
                            (Eq.trans
                              (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
                                Eq.refl
                                  (Fin.cons z' (fun j => DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E j).source) x))
                              (Fin.sum_univ_succ
                                (Fin.cons z' fun j => DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E j).source)))
                            (congr
                              (congrArg HAdd.hAdd
                                (Fin.cons_zero z' fun j => DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E j).source))
                              (Finset.sum_congr (Eq.refl Finset.univ) fun x a =>
                                Fin.cons_succ z' (fun j => DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E j).source)
                                  x)))))
                      (Eq.mpr
                        (id
                          (congrArg (fun _a => _a = z' + ∑ j, DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E j).source)
                            (Eq.symm hx)))
                        (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => x = _a) (Eq.symm hx'))) (Eq.refl x)));
                  Exists.casesOn
                    (DiamondIntegral.Section7.Refinement.refine_matrix DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.refine2
                      DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.eq_zero_of_add_eq_zero (n + 1) (m + 1) a b hab)
                    fun c h =>
                    And.casesOn h fun hrow hcol =>
                      have hrow' := fun i =>
                        have this := hrow i.succ;
                        Eq.symm
                          (Eq.mp
                            (congrArg (Eq (∑ j, c i.succ j))
                              (Fin.cons_succ z (fun i => DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (D i).source) i))
                            this);
                      have hcol' := fun j =>
                        have this := hcol j.succ;
                        Eq.symm
                          (Eq.mp
                            (congrArg (Eq (∑ i, c i j.succ))
                              (Fin.cons_succ z' (fun j => DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E j).source) j))
                            this);
                      (fun Dsp x =>
                          (fun Esp x_1 =>
                              have hsrc := fun i j =>
                                Eq.trans ((fun i => (x i).left) i j.succ) (Eq.symm ((fun j => (x_1 j).left) j i.succ));
                              (fun w x_2 =>
                                  have hz :=
                                    have this := hrow 0;
                                    Eq.symm
                                      (Eq.mp
                                        (congr (congrArg Eq (Fin.sum_univ_succ (c 0)))
                                          (Fin.cons_zero z fun i =>
                                            DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (D i).source))
                                        this);
                                  have hz' :=
                                    have this := hcol 0;
                                    Eq.symm
                                      (Eq.mp
                                        (congr (congrArg Eq (Fin.sum_univ_succ fun i => c i 0))
                                          (Fin.cons_zero z' fun j =>
                                            DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (E j).source))
                                        this);
                                  Exists.intro
                                    (c 0 0 + ∑ j, DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (Esp j 0).annularObject +
                                        ∑ i, DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls (Dsp i 0).annularObject +
                                      ∑ i, ∑ j, w i j)
                                    ⟨have hy2 :=
                                        Eq.mpr
                                          (id
                                            (congrArg
                                              (fun _a =>
                                                _a =
                                                  c 0 0 + ∑ j, c 0 j.succ +
                                                      ∑ i,
                                                        DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                                                          (Dsp i 0).annularObject +
                                                    ∑ i,
                                                      ∑ j,
                                                        DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                                                          (Dsp i j.succ).annularObject)
                                              hy))
                                          (Eq.mpr
                                            (id
                                              (congrArg
                                                (fun _a =>
                                                  _a +
                                                      ∑ i,
                                                        DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                                                          (D i).annularObject =
                                                    c 0 0 + ∑ j, c 0 j.succ +
                                                        ∑ i,
                                                          DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                                                            (Dsp i 0).annularObject +
                                                      ∑ i,
                                                        ∑ j,
                                                          DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls

  DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join n m u y i ∈ Set.range u ∪ Set.range y
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.join_mem_range.{u_1} : ∀ {α : Type u_1} (n m : ℕ) (u : Fin m → α)

  DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join n m u y i ∈ Set.range u ∪ Set.range y :=

  Nat.recAux (motive := fun m =>
    ∀ (u : Fin m → α) (i : Fin (n + m)),
      DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join n m u y i ∈ Set.range u ∪ Set.range y)
    (fun u i => Or.inr (Exists.intro i rfl))
    (fun m ih u i =>
      Fin.cases (Or.inl (Exists.intro 0 rfl))
        (fun i' =>
          Or.casesOn (ih (Fin.tail u) i') (fun h => Exists.casesOn h fun a ha => Or.inl (Exists.intro a.succ ha))

    m u i

DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.index_cases : ∀ (m : ℕ) (i : Fin (1 + m)),
  (∃ i', i = DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 m i') ∨
    i = DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 m 0
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.index_cases : ∀ (m : ℕ) (i : Fin (1 + m)),
  (∃ i', i = DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 m i') ∨
    i = DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 m 0 :=
fun m i =>
  have h :=
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.join_mem_range 1 m
      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 m)
      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 m) i;
  have hi := congrFun (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join_reconstruct 1 m id) i;
  Or.casesOn
    (Eq.mp
      (congrArg
        (fun _a =>
          _a ∈
            Set.range (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 m) ∪
              Set.range (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 m))
        hi)
      h)
    (fun h => Exists.casesOn h fun a ha => Or.inl (Exists.intro a (Eq.symm ha))) fun h =>
    Exists.casesOn h fun a ha =>
      Or.inr
        (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 m 0) (Eq.symm ha)))
          ((fun n m a a_1 e_a => e_a ▸ Eq.refl (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex n m a)) 1 m a 0

  [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] {n : ℕ} {u u' : Fin n → M} {t t' : M},

theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.gpt_injective : ∀ {M : Type}
  [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] {n : ℕ} {u u' : Fin n → M} {t t' : M},

    u = u' ∧ t = t' :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] {n} {u u'} {t t'} h =>
  ⟨Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = u') (Eq.symm (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.gpt_head u t))))
      (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 n = u') h))
        (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = u') (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.gpt_head u' t')))
          (Eq.refl u'))),
    Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = t') (Eq.symm (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.gpt_last u t))))
      (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 n 0) = t') h))
        (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = t') (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.gpt_last u' t')))

    r τ u t =>

    (Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg
          (fun _a =>
            _a ∈ r.domain ∧

                  DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.gpt u t ∘

                (congrArg
                  (fun _a =>
                    DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.gpt u (τ.aperture u) ∈
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section4.BlowupChart.graphChart r τ).domain ∧
                        SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Proper ![s] ∧

                      u ∈ r.domain ∧
                        SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.NonzeroRV s ∧

                (id
                  (congrArg
                    (fun _a =>
                      _a ∧
                          SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Proper ![s] ∧
