                              SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue s ≤
                                SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (τ.aperture u) ↔
                        u ∈ r.domain ∧
                          SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.NonzeroRV s ∧
                            1 < SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue s ∧
                              SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue s ≤
                                SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (τ.aperture u))
                    (propext (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.mem_graph_iff r τ u (τ.aperture u)))))
                {
                  mp := fun a =>
                    And.casesOn a fun left right =>
                      And.casesOn left fun hu right_1 =>
                        And.casesOn right fun hp right =>
                          And.casesOn right fun h1 h2 => ⟨hu, ⟨⟨(hp 0).left, (hp 0).right⟩, ⟨h1, h2⟩⟩⟩,
                  mpr := fun a =>
                    And.casesOn a fun hu right =>
                      And.casesOn right fun hs right =>
                        And.casesOn right fun h1 h2 =>
                          ⟨⟨hu, rfl⟩,
                            ⟨fun i =>
                              List.Mem.casesOn (motive := fun a x =>
                                List.finRange 1 = a →
                                  Fintype.complete i ≍ x →
                                    SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRV (![s] i) ∧
                                      ![s] i ≠ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero)
                                (Fintype.complete i)
                                (fun as h =>
                                  List.cons.noConfusion h fun head_eq =>
                                    Eq.ndrec (motive := fun i =>
                                      ∀ (this : i ∈ Fintype.elems),
                                        [] ≍ as →
                                          this ≍ List.Mem.head as →
                                            SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRV (![s] i) ∧
                                              ![s] i ≠ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero)
                                      (fun this tail_eq =>
                                        Eq.ndrec (motive := fun as =>
                                          this ≍ List.Mem.head as →
                                            SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRV
                                                (![s] ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl 1⟩)) ∧
                                              ![s] ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl 1⟩) ≠
                                                SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero)
                                          (fun h => ⟨hs.left, hs.right⟩) (eq_of_heq tail_eq))
                                      (eq_of_heq head_eq) (Fintype.complete i))
                                (fun b {as} a h =>
                                  List.cons.noConfusion h fun head_eq =>
                                    Eq.ndrec (motive := fun b =>
                                      [] ≍ as →
                                        Fintype.complete i ≍ List.Mem.tail b a →
                                          SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRV (![s] i) ∧
                                            ![s] i ≠ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero)
                                      (fun tail_eq =>
                                        Eq.ndrec (motive := fun {as} =>
                                          ∀ (a : List.Mem i as),
                                            Fintype.complete i ≍ List.Mem.tail ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl 1⟩) a →
                                              SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRV (![s] i) ∧
                                                ![s] i ≠ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero)
                                          (fun a h =>
                                            List.Mem.casesOn (motive := fun a_1 x =>
                                              [] = a_1 →
                                                a ≍ x →
                                                  SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRV (![s] i) ∧
                                                    ![s] i ≠ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero)
                                              a (fun as h => False.elim (noConfusion_of_Nat List.ctorIdx h))
                                              (fun b {as} a_1 h => False.elim (noConfusion_of_Nat List.ctorIdx h))
                                              (Eq.refl []) (HEq.refl a))
                                          (eq_of_heq tail_eq) a)
                                      (eq_of_heq head_eq))
                                (Eq.refl (List.finRange 1)) (HEq.refl (Fintype.complete i)),
                              ⟨h1, h2⟩⟩⟩ }))))))

  Eq.mpr
    (id
      (congrArg
        (fun _a =>
          DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.isoMap (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredIsomorphisms.symm F) _a =
            u)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.isoMap_on F ⟨u, hu⟩)))

      DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.isoMap_on (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredIsomorphisms.symm F)

@DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.cancel_gamma : ∀ {G : Type u_1} [inst : CommGroup G] (a t s : G),
  a * t * (s * t⁻¹) = a * s
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.cancel_gamma.{u_1} : ∀ {G : Type u_1} [inst : CommGroup G]
  (a t s : G), a * t * (s * t⁻¹) = a * s :=
fun {G} [CommGroup G] a t s =>
  Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => a * t * _a = a * s) (mul_comm s t⁻¹)))
    (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = a * s) (Eq.symm (mul_assoc (a * t) t⁻¹ s))))
      (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a * s = a * s) (mul_assoc a t t⁻¹)))
        (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => a * _a * s = a * s) (mul_inv_cancel t)))
          (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a * s = a * s) (mul_one a))) (Eq.refl (a * s))))))

  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]

theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.elementaryDiamond : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {k : ℕ},
  DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.ElementaryDiamond B k :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {k}
    D E h =>
  Nonempty.casesOn (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls_eq_iff.mp h) fun F =>
    id
      (fun F =>
        have hτ := D.aperture.graph_definable;
        have hσF :=
          DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.scalarDef_comp (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.isoMap_graph F)
            E.aperture.graph_definable fun x hu => DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.isoMap_mem F hu;
        let I :=
          {u |
            u ∈ D.chart.domain ∧
              SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (D.aperture.aperture u) ≤
                SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                  (E.aperture.aperture (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.isoMap F u))};
        have hI :=
          DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.le_definable D.chart.domain_definable hτ hσF
            (fun u hu => D.aperture.nonzero u hu) fun u hu =>
            E.aperture.nonzero (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.isoMap F u)
              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.isoMap_mem F hu);
        have hJ := DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image_definable F I hI;
        have hA1 :=
          DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.step_of_le
            (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumRes D I hI)
            (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumRes E (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image F I)
              hJ)
            (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.restrictIso F I hI) fun u hu =>
            have he :=
              DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.isoMap_on
                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.restrictIso F I hI) ⟨u, hu⟩;
            Eq.mpr
              (id
                (congrArg
                  (fun _a =>
                    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                        ((DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumRes D I hI).aperture.aperture u) ≤
                      SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                        ((DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumRes E
                                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image F I) hJ).aperture.aperture
                          _a))
                  he))
              hu.right.right;
        have hA2 :=
          DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.step_of_le
            (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumCres E (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image F I)
              hJ)
            (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumCres D I hI)
            (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.complIso D E F I hI) fun v hv =>
            have he :=
              Eq.trans
                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.isoMap_on
                  (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.complIso D E F I hI) ⟨v, hv⟩)
                rfl;
            Eq.mpr
              (id
                (congrArg
                  (fun _a =>
                    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                        ((DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumCres E
                                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image F I) hJ).aperture.aperture
                          v) ≤
                      SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                        ((DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumCres D I hI).aperture.aperture _a))
                  he))
              (have hu :=
                DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.isoMap_mem
                  (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredIsomorphisms.symm F) hv.left;
              have hnot := fun hi =>
                hv.right
                  (Exists.intro
                    (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.isoMap
                      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredIsomorphisms.symm F) v)
                    ⟨⟨hu, hi⟩, DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.isoMap_isoMap_symm F hv.left⟩);
              have hlt := lt_of_not_ge fun hle => hnot ⟨hu, hle⟩;
              LT.lt.le
                (Eq.mp
                  (congrArg
                    (fun _a =>
                      SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (E.aperture.aperture _a) <
                        SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                          (D.aperture.aperture (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.finv F v)))
                    (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryComparison.isoMap_isoMap_symm F hv.left))
                  hlt));
        Exists.intro
          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumRes E (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image F I)
                  hJ).annularObject +
            DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumCres D I hI).annularObject)
          ⟨Eq.mpr
              (id
                (congrArg
                  (fun _a =>
                    DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.Step _a
                      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumRes E
                              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image F I) hJ).annularObject +
                        DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumCres D I hI).annularObject))
                  (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cls_annular_split D I hI)))
              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.step_add hA1
                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.step_refl
                  (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                    (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumCres D I hI).annularObject))),
            Eq.mpr
              (id
                (congrArg
                  (fun _a =>
                    DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.Step _a
                      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumRes E
                              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image F I) hJ).annularObject +
                        DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.cls
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.datumCres D I hI).annularObject))
                  (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.cls_annular_split E
                    (DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.image F I) hJ)))
              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.step_add

  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]

  (T : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Substructure M) (hsmall : Cardinal.mk ↑↑T < κ)

  (hI :
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase (DiamondIntegral.Section7.LiftClasses.BT T)
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) I),

    h00 {κ} hκ hs T hsmall hΓ c hb hn I hI =>
  let D :=
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.rawDefSet hc (c.domain ∩ I)
      (SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase.inter c.domain_definable hI);
  have hD := fun x h => h.left;
  have hU := (DiamondIntegral.Section7.LiftClasses.dbOf hc h00 hκ hs T hsmall hΓ c hb hn).property;
  have h :=

    a i =>

    DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVGraphComposition.graph_compose_definable { real := S, gamma := ∅ } a.domain
      (SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.sortedUniverse fun x =>
        SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)
      a.coordinate (fun c => c ∘ fun x => i) a.coordinate_definable

          SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)

          (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)

            fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv))
        fun x => i)
      fun x hx l => (a.coordinate_proper x hx l).left;

    funext fun x =>
      funext fun l =>
        List.Mem.casesOn (motive := fun a_1 x_1 =>
          List.finRange 1 = a_1 →

            List.cons.noConfusion h fun head_eq =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun b =>

                  Fintype.complete l ≍ List.Mem.tail b a_1 →

                        ((fun c => c ∘ fun x => i) ∘ a.coordinate) x l = ![a.coordinate x i] l)
                    (fun a_2 h =>

                        a_2 (fun as h => False.elim (noConfusion_of_Nat List.ctorIdx h))
                        (fun b {as} a_3 h => False.elim (noConfusion_of_Nat List.ctorIdx h)) (Eq.refl [])
                        (HEq.refl a_2))
                    (eq_of_heq tail_eq) a_1)
                (eq_of_heq head_eq))
          (Eq.refl (List.finRange 1)) (HEq.refl (Fintype.complete l));

    (congrArg
      (fun _a =>
        SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase { real := S, gamma := ∅ }

@DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBounds.coordinateRV_graph : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  (hc : CanonicalStructure.IsCanonical) {S : Set M} {k : ℕ}

    (DiamondIntegral.Section2.rvScalarFunctionGraph
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.toObject hc a).domain.carrier
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBounds.coordinateRV a i))
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBounds.coordinateRV_graph : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  (hc : CanonicalStructure.IsCanonical) {S : Set M} {k : ℕ}
  (a : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Chart { real := S, gamma := ∅ } k) (i : Fin k),
  S.Definable SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV
    (DiamondIntegral.Section2.rvScalarFunctionGraph
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.toObject hc a).domain.carrier
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBounds.coordinateRV a i)) :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] hc {S}
    {k} a i =>
  let σ := DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join 1 a.arity Sum.inl ![Sum.inr 0];
  have h :=
    Set.Definable.preimage_comp σ
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.base_real
        (DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBounds.coordinate_scalar_definable a i));
  have hh := fun z =>
    funext fun l =>
      of_eq_true
        (Eq.trans
          (congrFun'
            (congrArg Eq
              (congrArg z (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join_head 1 a.arity Sum.inl ![Sum.inr 0] l)))
            (z (Sum.inl l)))
          (eq_self (z (Sum.inl l))));
  have ht := fun z =>
    funext fun l =>
      List.Mem.casesOn (motive := fun a_1 x =>
        List.finRange 1 = a_1 →
          Fintype.complete l ≍ x →
            ((z ∘ σ) ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 a.arity) l = ![z (Sum.inr 0)] l)
        (Fintype.complete l)
        (fun as h =>
          List.cons.noConfusion h fun head_eq =>
            Eq.ndrec (motive := fun l =>
              ∀ (this : l ∈ Fintype.elems),
                [] ≍ as →
                  this ≍ List.Mem.head as →
                    ((z ∘ σ) ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 a.arity) l = ![z (Sum.inr 0)] l)
              (fun this tail_eq =>
                Eq.ndrec (motive := fun as =>
                  this ≍ List.Mem.head as →
                    ((z ∘ σ) ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 a.arity)
                        ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl 1⟩) =
                      ![z (Sum.inr 0)] ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl 1⟩))
                  (fun h =>
                    Eq.mpr
                      (id
                        (congrFun'
                          (congrArg Eq
                            (congrArg z
                              (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join_tail 1 a.arity Sum.inl ![Sum.inr 0]
                                ⟨0, Nat.le_refl 1⟩)))
                          (![z (Sum.inr 0)] ⟨0, Nat.le_refl 1⟩)))
                      (Eq.refl (z (![Sum.inr 0] ⟨0, Nat.le_refl 1⟩))))
                  (eq_of_heq tail_eq))
              (eq_of_heq head_eq) (Fintype.complete l))
        (fun b {as} a_1 h =>
          List.cons.noConfusion h fun head_eq =>
            Eq.ndrec (motive := fun b =>
              [] ≍ as →
                Fintype.complete l ≍ List.Mem.tail b a_1 →
                  ((z ∘ σ) ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 a.arity) l = ![z (Sum.inr 0)] l)
              (fun tail_eq =>
                Eq.ndrec (motive := fun {as} =>
                  ∀ (a_2 : List.Mem l as),
                    Fintype.complete l ≍ List.Mem.tail ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl 1⟩) a_2 →
                      ((z ∘ σ) ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 a.arity) l = ![z (Sum.inr 0)] l)
                  (fun a_2 h =>
                    List.Mem.casesOn (motive := fun a_3 x =>
                      [] = a_3 →
                        a_2 ≍ x →
                          ((z ∘ σ) ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 a.arity) l =
                            ![z (Sum.inr 0)] l)
                      a_2 (fun as h => False.elim (noConfusion_of_Nat List.ctorIdx h))
                      (fun b {as} a_3 h => False.elim (noConfusion_of_Nat List.ctorIdx h)) (Eq.refl []) (HEq.refl a_2))
                  (eq_of_heq tail_eq) a_1)
              (eq_of_heq head_eq))
        (Eq.refl (List.finRange 1)) (HEq.refl (Fintype.complete l));
  have hset :=
    Set.ext fun z =>
      {
        mp := fun a_1 =>
          Exists.casesOn a_1 fun u h =>
            And.casesOn h fun hu right =>
              And.casesOn right fun hzx hzy =>
                have hx := hu;
                id
                  (Eq.mpr
                    (id
                      (congrArg
                        (fun _a =>
                          _a ∈ a.domain ∧
                            ![a.coordinate _a i] =
                              (z ∘ σ) ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 a.arity)
                        (hh z)))
                    (Eq.mpr
                      (id (congrArg (fun _a => z ∘ Sum.inl ∈ a.domain ∧ ![a.coordinate (z ∘ Sum.inl) i] = _a) (ht z)))
                      (have hzx' := Eq.symm hzx;
                      Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a ∈ a.domain ∧ ![a.coordinate _a i] = ![z (Sum.inr 0)]) hzx'))
                        ⟨hx,
                          Eq.mpr
                            (id
                              (congrArg
                                (fun _a => ![a.coordinate (SpecialTransformations.Section4.rvValues u) i] = ![_a])
                                (Eq.symm hzy)))
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg
                                  (fun _a => ![a.coordinate (SpecialTransformations.Section4.rvValues u) i] = ![_a])
                                  (DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBounds.coordinateRV_val a i u hx)))
                              (Eq.refl ![a.coordinate (SpecialTransformations.Section4.rvValues u) i]))⟩))),
        mpr := fun a_1 =>
          And.casesOn a_1 fun hz1 hz2 =>
            have hz0 :=
              congrFun
                (Eq.mp (congrArg (fun _a => (fun x => ![a.coordinate x i]) (z ∘ Sum.inl) = _a) (ht z))
                  (Eq.mp
                    (congrArg
                      (fun _a =>
                        (fun x => ![a.coordinate x i]) _a =
                          (fun g => g ∘ σ) z ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 a.arity)

            Exists.intro
              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.rvTuple x
                (SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase.proper a.domain_definable x hx))
              ⟨hx, rfl⟩ };
    Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => S.Definable SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV _a) he)) h;
  have hb := fun i =>

      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.toObject hc a).domain.carrier hU

        Exists.intro β fun t a_1 =>
          Exists.casesOn (motive := fun x =>
            ∀ (i : Fin k),
              ↑β⁻¹ ≤ SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.vrv (t i) ∧
                SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.vrv (t i) ≤ ↑β)
            a_1 fun u h =>
            Eq.ndrec (motive := fun t =>
              ∀ (i : Fin k),
                ↑β⁻¹ ≤ SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.vrv (t i) ∧
                  SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.vrv (t i) ≤ ↑β)
              (fun i =>
                have hx := u.property;
                have h := hγ i (↑u) u.property;
                have hv :=
                  Subtype.ext
                    (Eq.mpr
                      (id
                        (congrArg
                          (fun _a => ↑((DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.toObject hc a).value u i) = _a)

                        ↑β⁻¹ ≤ SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.vrv _a ∧

                  ⟨le_trans (inv_le_inv_iff.mpr (hβ i)) h.left, le_trans h.right (hβ i)⟩)

theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.noNames_real : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M]
  [inst_1 : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] (hc : CanonicalStructure.IsCanonical) {S : Set M}
  {Q : SpecialTransformations.DefinableGeometry.Space SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV M} {A : Set Q.Carrier},
  DiamondIntegral.Section3.GammaGroundDefinable hc { real := S, gamma := ∅ } Q A → Q.Definable S A :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] hc {S}
    {Q} {A} hA =>
  Exists.casesOn hA fun k h =>
    Exists.casesOn h fun γ h =>
      And.casesOn h fun hγ hA =>
        Nat.casesAuxOn (motive := fun a => k = a → Q.Definable S A) k
          (fun h =>
            Eq.ndrec (motive := fun k =>
              ∀ (γ : Fin k → SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma),
                (∀ (i : Fin k), γ i ∈ { real := S, gamma := ∅ }.gamma) →
                  DiamondIntegral.Section3.ImaginaryDefinable
                      ((DiamondIntegral.Section3.volumeGammaSpace hc).power (Fin k)) γ Q { real := S, gamma := ∅ }.real
                      A →
                    Q.Definable S A)
              (fun γ hγ hA =>
                let c := ⟨fun x => x.1.elim0, fun i => Fin.elim0 i⟩;
                have hp := funext fun i => Fin.elim0 i;
                have h := DiamondIntegral.Section3.ImaginaryDefinable.over_real_code hA c hp;
                have hS :=
                  have he :=
                    Set.ext fun x =>
                      Eq.mpr
                        (id
                          (Eq.trans
                            (Eq.trans
                              (congr (congrArg Iff DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.noNames_real._simp_1_1)
                                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.noNames_real._simp_1_2 x))
                              (iff_false (∃ y, ↑c y = x)))
                            DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.noNames_real._simp_1_3))
                        fun y => Fin.elim0 y.1;
                  id
                    (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => S ∪ _a = S) he))
                      (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = S) (Set.union_empty S))) (Eq.refl S)));
                Eq.mp (congrArg (fun _a => Q.Definable _a A) hS) h)
              (Eq.symm h) γ hγ hA)
          (fun k_1 h =>
            Eq.ndrec (motive := fun k =>
              ∀ (γ : Fin k → SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma),
                (∀ (i : Fin k), γ i ∈ { real := S, gamma := ∅ }.gamma) →
                  DiamondIntegral.Section3.ImaginaryDefinable
                      ((DiamondIntegral.Section3.volumeGammaSpace hc).power (Fin k)) γ Q { real := S, gamma := ∅ }.real
                      A →
                    Q.Definable S A)
              (fun γ hγ hA => False.elim (hγ 0)) (Eq.symm h) γ hγ hA)
          (Eq.refl k)

theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.isoRaw_mem : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M]
  [inst_1 : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {S : Set M} {k : ℕ}
  {a b : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Chart { real := S, gamma := ∅ } k}
  (F : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Iso a b) (x : Fin a.arity → M) (hx : x ∈ a.domain),
  DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.isoRaw F x = ↑(F.equiv ⟨x, hx⟩) :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {S} {k}
    {a b} F x hx =>
  of_eq_true
    (Eq.trans (congrFun' (congrArg Eq (dite_eq_left_of_eq_true (eq_true hx))) ↑(F.equiv ⟨x, hx⟩))
      (eq_self ↑(F.equiv ⟨x, of_eq_true (eq_true hx)⟩)))

    (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn a.domain (DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.isoRaw F))
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.isoRaw_graph : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M]
  [inst_1 : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {S : Set M} {k : ℕ}
  {a b : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Chart { real := S, gamma := ∅ } k}
  (F : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Iso a b),
  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase { real := S, gamma := ∅ }
    (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)
    (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn a.domain (DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.isoRaw F)) :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {S} {k}
    {a b} F =>
  have he :=
    Set.ext fun w =>
      Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg
            (fun _a =>
              _a ↔ w ∈ {w | ∃ u, w = DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join b.arity a.arity ↑u ↑(F.equiv u)})
            (propext
              (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVRealProducts.graphOn_exists a.domain
                (DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.isoRaw F) w))))
        {
          mp := fun a_1 =>
            Exists.casesOn a_1 fun x h =>
              And.casesOn h fun hx right =>
                Eq.symm right ▸
                  Exists.intro ⟨x, hx⟩
                    (Eq.mpr
                      (id
                        (congrArg
                          (fun _a =>
                            DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join b.arity a.arity x _a =
                              DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join b.arity a.arity ↑⟨x, hx⟩
                                ↑(F.equiv ⟨x, hx⟩))
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.isoRaw_mem F x hx)))
                      (Eq.refl (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join b.arity a.arity x ↑(F.equiv ⟨x, hx⟩)))),
          mpr := fun a_1 =>
            Exists.casesOn a_1 fun u h =>
              Eq.symm h ▸
                Exists.intro ↑u
                  ⟨u.property,
                    Eq.mpr
                      (id
                        (congrArg
                          (fun _a =>
                            DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join b.arity a.arity ↑u ↑(F.equiv u) =
                              DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join b.arity a.arity (↑u) _a)
                          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.isoRaw_mem F (↑u) u.property)))
                      (Eq.refl (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join b.arity a.arity ↑u ↑(F.equiv u)))⟩ };
  Eq.mpr
    (id
      (congrArg
        (fun _a =>
          SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase { real := S, gamma := ∅ }
            (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) _a)
        he))
    F.graph_definable

  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]

      funext fun i =>
        List.Mem.casesOn (motive := fun a x =>
          List.finRange (Nat.succ 0) = a → Fintype.complete i ≍ x → (u ∘ ![p]) i = ![u p] i) (Fintype.complete i)
          (fun as h =>
            List.cons.noConfusion h fun head_eq =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun i =>
                ∀ (this : i ∈ Fintype.elems), [] ≍ as → this ≍ List.Mem.head as → (u ∘ ![p]) i = ![u p] i)
                (fun this tail_eq =>
                  Eq.ndrec (motive := fun as =>
                    this ≍ List.Mem.head as →
                      (u ∘ ![p]) ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl (Nat.succ 0)⟩) =
                        ![u p] ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl (Nat.succ 0)⟩))
                    (fun h => Eq.refl ((u ∘ ![p]) ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl (Nat.succ 0)⟩))) (eq_of_heq tail_eq))
                (eq_of_heq head_eq) (Fintype.complete i))
          (fun b {as} a h =>
            List.cons.noConfusion h fun head_eq =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun b => [] ≍ as → Fintype.complete i ≍ List.Mem.tail b a → (u ∘ ![p]) i = ![u p] i)
                (fun tail_eq =>
                  Eq.ndrec (motive := fun {as} =>
                    ∀ (a : List.Mem i as),
                      Fintype.complete i ≍ List.Mem.tail ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl (Nat.succ 0)⟩) a →
                        (u ∘ ![p]) i = ![u p] i)
                    (fun a h =>
                      List.Mem.casesOn (motive := fun a_1 x => [] = a_1 → a ≍ x → (u ∘ ![p]) i = ![u p] i) a
                        (fun as h => False.elim (noConfusion_of_Nat List.ctorIdx h))
                        (fun b {as} a_1 h => False.elim (noConfusion_of_Nat List.ctorIdx h)) (Eq.refl []) (HEq.refl a))
                    (eq_of_heq tail_eq) a)
                (eq_of_heq head_eq))
          (Eq.refl (List.finRange (Nat.succ 0))) (HEq.refl (Fintype.complete i));
  id
    (Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg
          (fun _a =>
            SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
              (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)
              (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn I _a))
          (Eq.symm he)))
      h)

theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.le_definable : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {n : ℕ} {I : Set (Fin n → M)},
  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) I →
    ∀ {g h : (Fin n → M) → M},
      DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.ScalarDef B I g →
        DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.ScalarDef B I h →
          (∀ u ∈ I, SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.NonzeroRV (g u)) →
            (∀ u ∈ I, SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.NonzeroRV (h u)) →
              SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
                (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)
                {u |
                  u ∈ I ∧
                    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (g u) ≤
                      SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (h u)} :=
fun {M} [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M]
    [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {n} {I} hI {g h} hg hh hgn hhn =>
  have hdiff :=
    DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.difference_definable B I
      {u |
        u ∈ I ∧
          SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (h u) <
            SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (g u)}
      hI (DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.lt_definable hI hh hg hhn hgn);
  Eq.mpr
    (eq_of_heq
      ((fun M sM hM B n sn A A' e'_7 =>
          Eq.casesOn (motive := fun a x =>
            A' = a →
              e'_7 ≍ x →
                SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A ≍
                  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A')
            e'_7
            (fun h =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun A' =>
                ∀ (e_7 : A = A'),
                  e_7 ≍ Eq.refl A →
                    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A ≍
                      SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A')
                (fun e_7 h => HEq.refl (SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A))
                (Eq.symm h) e'_7)
            (Eq.refl A') (HEq.refl e'_7))
        M inst hM B n (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)
        {u |
          u ∈ I ∧
            SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (g u) ≤
              SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (h u)}
        (I \
          {u |
            u ∈ I ∧
              SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (h u) <
                SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (g u)})
        (Set.ext fun u =>
          { mp := fun a => And.casesOn a fun hu hle => ⟨hu, fun hlt => absurd hle (not_le.mpr hlt.right)⟩,

@DiamondIntegral.Section7.Remark74.aperture_value_family_definable : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {k : ℕ} {S : Set M} (r : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Chart { real := S, gamma := ∅ } k)
  (τ : DiamondIntegral.Section4.AnnularBlowup.Aperture r),
  S.Definable SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV
    (DiamondIntegral.Section2.rawGammaFamilyGraph r.domain fun u =>
      {SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (τ.aperture u)})
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.Remark74.aperture_value_family_definable : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {k : ℕ} {S : Set M} (r : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Chart { real := S, gamma := ∅ } k)
  (τ : DiamondIntegral.Section4.AnnularBlowup.Aperture r),
  S.Definable SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV
    (DiamondIntegral.Section2.rawGammaFamilyGraph r.domain fun u =>
      {SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (τ.aperture u)}) :=
fun {M} [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
    {k} {S} r τ =>
  let I :=
    {w |
        w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 r.arity ∈ r.domain ∧
          w ∈
            SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.sortedUniverse fun x =>
              SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv} \
      DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.coordEq (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 r.arity 0)
        SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero;
  have hI :=
    DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVChartRestrictions.difference_definable { real := S, gamma := ∅ }
      {w |
        w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 r.arity ∈ r.domain ∧
          w ∈
            SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.sortedUniverse fun x =>
              SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv}
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.coordEq (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 r.arity 0)
        SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero)
      (SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase.preimage_real r.domain_definable
        (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 r.arity))
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.coordEq_definable
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 r.arity 0)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVNamedTuples.zero_definable { real := S, gamma := ∅ }));
  have hz := fun w hw =>
    ⟨hw.left.right (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 r.arity 0), fun h =>
      hw.right ((DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.mem_coordEq hw.left.right).mpr h)⟩;
  have hhead := fun w hw => hw.left.left;
  have hτ :=
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.scalarDef_comp
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.tupleDef_coord hI
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 r.arity))
      τ.graph_definable hhead;
  have hτn := fun w hw => τ.nonzero (w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 r.arity) (hhead hw);
  have h1 :=
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.le_definable hI
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.scalarDef_coord hI
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 r.arity 0))
      hτ hz hτn;
  have h2 :=
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.le_definable hI hτ
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.scalarDef_coord hI
        (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 r.arity 0))
      hτn hz;
  have hE :=
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.MeasuredRVBridge.base_real
      (SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase.inter h1 h2);
  let σ := DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join 1 r.arity Sum.inl Sum.inr;
  have hpre := Set.Definable.preimage_comp σ hE;
  Eq.mpr
    (eq_of_heq
      ((fun M A L inst α s s' e'_6 =>
          Eq.casesOn (motive := fun a x => s' = a → e'_6 ≍ x → A.Definable L s ≍ A.Definable L s') e'_6
            (fun h =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun s' => ∀ (e_6 : s = s'), e_6 ≍ Eq.refl s → A.Definable L s ≍ A.Definable L s')
                (fun e_6 h => HEq.refl (A.Definable L s)) (Eq.symm h) e'_6)
            (Eq.refl s') (HEq.refl e'_6))
        M S SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV inst (Fin r.arity ⊕ Fin 1)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section2.rawGammaFamilyGraph r.domain fun u =>
          {SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (τ.aperture u)})
        ((fun g => g ∘ σ) ⁻¹'
          ({u |
              u ∈ I ∧
                SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                    (u (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 r.arity 0)) ≤
                  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                    (τ.aperture (u ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 r.arity))} ∩
            {u |
              u ∈ I ∧
                SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                    (τ.aperture (u ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 r.arity)) ≤
                  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                    (u (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 r.arity 0))}))
        (Set.ext fun z =>
          have hzh :=
            funext fun i =>
              of_eq_true
                (Eq.trans
                  (congrFun'
                    (congrArg Eq
                      (congrArg z (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join_head 1 r.arity Sum.inl Sum.inr i)))
                    (z (Sum.inl i)))
                  (eq_self (z (Sum.inl i))));
          have hzt :=
            of_eq_true
              (Eq.trans
                (congrFun'
                  (congrArg Eq
                    (congrArg z (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join_tail 1 r.arity Sum.inl Sum.inr 0)))
                  (z (Sum.inr 0)))
                (eq_self (z (Sum.inr 0))));
          {
            mp := fun a =>
              And.casesOn a fun hd right =>
                And.casesOn right fun hn hv =>
                  have hsort := fun i =>
                    Or.casesOn (DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.block_cases 1 r.arity i)
                      (fun h =>
                        Exists.casesOn h fun i' h =>
                          Eq.symm h ▸
                            id
                              (Eq.mpr
                                (id
                                  (congrArg (fun _a => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRV _a)
                                    (have this := congrFun hzh i';
                                    this)))
                                (SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase.proper
                                  r.domain_definable (z ∘ Sum.inl) hd i')))
                      fun h =>
                      Exists.casesOn h fun i' h =>
                        Eq.symm h ▸
                          id
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg
                                  (fun _a =>
                                    SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRV
                                      ((z ∘ σ) (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 r.arity _a)))
                                  (Subsingleton.elim i' 0)))
                              (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRV _a) hzt))
                                hn.left));
                  have hIz :=
                    ⟨⟨Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a ∈ r.domain) hzh)) hd, hsort⟩, fun hc =>
                      hn.right
                        (Eq.mpr (id (congrArg (fun _a => _a = SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero) (Eq.symm hzt)))
                          ((DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.mem_coordEq hsort).mp hc))⟩;
                  have hv' :=
                    Eq.mpr
                      (id
                        (congrArg
                          (fun _a =>
                            SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue _a =
                              SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                                (τ.aperture ((z ∘ σ) ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 r.arity)))
                          hzt))
                      (Eq.mpr
                        (id
                          (congrArg
                            (fun _a =>
                              SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (z (Sum.inr 0)) =
                                SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (τ.aperture _a))
                            hzh))
                        hv);
                  ⟨⟨hIz, Eq.le hv'⟩, ⟨hIz, Eq.ge hv'⟩⟩,
            mpr := fun a =>
              And.casesOn a fun left right =>
                And.casesOn left fun hIz hle =>
                  And.casesOn right fun left hge =>
                    have hd := hzh ▸ hIz.left.left;
                    have hn := hzt ▸ hz (z ∘ σ) hIz;
                    ⟨hd,
                      ⟨hn,
                        id
                          (Eq.mpr
                            (id
                              (congrArg
                                (fun _a =>
                                  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue _a =
                                    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                                      (τ.aperture (z ∘ Sum.inl)))
                                (Eq.symm hzt)))
                            (Eq.mpr
                              (id
                                (congrArg
                                  (fun _a =>
                                    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue
                                        ((z ∘ σ) (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 r.arity 0)) =
                                      SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (τ.aperture _a))
                                  (Eq.symm hzh)))
                              (le_antisymm hle hge)))⟩⟩ })))
    hpre

theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.Remark74.remark_7_4_named : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {k : ℕ},
  CanonicalStructure.IsCanonical →
    M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF00 →
      ∀ {S : Set M} (r : DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.Chart { real := S, gamma := ∅ } k)
        (τ : DiamondIntegral.Section4.AnnularBlowup.Aperture r),
        ∃ Δ,
          S.Definable SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV
              {z | z 0 ∈ SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.gammaPullback {Δ}} ∧
            1 ≤ Δ ∧ ∀ u ∈ r.domain, SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (τ.aperture u) ≤ Δ :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {k} hc
    h00 {S} r τ =>
  Exists.casesOn
    (DiamondIntegral.Section6.bounded_gamma_rows_named_bound hc h00 r.domain
      (fun u => {SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (τ.aperture u)})
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.Remark74.aperture_value_family_definable r τ)
      (Exists.intro (max τ.bound 1) fun u hu a ha =>
        Eq.symm (Eq.mp (congrArg (fun _a => _a) (propext Set.mem_singleton_iff)) ha) ▸
          have h1 := τ.nonnegative u hu;
          ⟨LE.le.trans (inv_le_one'.mpr (le_max_right τ.bound 1)) h1,
            LE.le.trans (τ.bounded u hu) (le_max_left τ.bound 1)⟩))
    fun δ h =>
    And.casesOn h fun hδ hb =>
      Exists.intro (max δ 1)
        ⟨DiamondIntegral.Section6.gamma_max_named δ 1 hδ (DiamondIntegral.Section6.gamma_one_named hc),
          ⟨le_max_right δ 1, fun u hu =>
            LE.le.trans
              (hb u hu (SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (τ.aperture u))
                  (Set.mem_singleton (SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.codeValue (τ.aperture u)))).right
              (le_max_left δ 1)⟩⟩

@DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.minusOne_definable : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base},
  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableClosureAtBase B SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.minusOne
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.minusOne_definable : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base},
  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableClosureAtBase B SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.minusOne :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
    {B} =>
  have h1 :=
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.definableClosureAtBase_function_symbol B
      SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Fn.vfOne Fin.elim0 (fun i => Fin.elim0 i) fun i => Fin.elim0 i;
  have h2 :=
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.definableClosureAtBase_function_symbol B
      SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Fn.vfNeg ![SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.iVFOne]
      (fun i =>
        List.Mem.casesOn (motive := fun a x =>
          List.finRange 1 = a →
            Fintype.complete i ≍ x →
              SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.sortEval
                (SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Fn.argSort SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Fn.vfNeg i)
                (![SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.iVFOne] i))
          (Fintype.complete i)
          (fun as h =>
            List.cons.noConfusion h fun head_eq =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun i =>
                ∀ (this : i ∈ Fintype.elems),
                  [] ≍ as →
                    this ≍ List.Mem.head as →
                      SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.sortEval
                        (SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Fn.argSort SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Fn.vfNeg i)
                        (![SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.iVFOne] i))
                (fun this tail_eq =>
                  Eq.ndrec (motive := fun as =>
                    this ≍ List.Mem.head as →
                      SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.sortEval
                        (SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Fn.argSort SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Fn.vfNeg
                          ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl 1⟩))
                        (![SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.iVFOne] ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl 1⟩)))
                    (fun h => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.vf_one_mem hM) (eq_of_heq tail_eq))
                (eq_of_heq head_eq) (Fintype.complete i))
          (fun b {as} a h =>
            List.cons.noConfusion h fun head_eq =>
              Eq.ndrec (motive := fun b =>
                [] ≍ as →
                  Fintype.complete i ≍ List.Mem.tail b a →
                    SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.sortEval
                      (SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Fn.argSort SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Fn.vfNeg i)
                      (![SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.iVFOne] i))
                (fun tail_eq =>
                  Eq.ndrec (motive := fun {as} =>
                    ∀ (a : List.Mem i as),
                      Fintype.complete i ≍ List.Mem.tail ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl 1⟩) a →
                        SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.sortEval

  Nat.recAux (motive := fun m =>

      DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join n m u y i ∈ Set.range u ∪ Set.range y)

      Fin.cases (Or.inl (Exists.intro 0 rfl))

    ∃ i', i = DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex n m i'
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.block_cases : ∀ (n m : ℕ) (i : Fin (n + m)),
  (∃ i', i = DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex n m i') ∨
    ∃ i', i = DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex n m i' :=
fun n m i =>
  have h :=
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.join_mem_range_blocks n m
      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex n m)
      (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex n m) i;
  have hi := congrFun (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.join_reconstruct n m id) i;
  Or.casesOn
    (Eq.mp
      (congrArg
        (fun _a =>
          _a ∈
            Set.range (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex n m) ∪
              Set.range (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex n m))
        hi)
      h)
    (fun h => Exists.casesOn h fun a ha => Or.inl (Exists.intro a (Eq.symm ha))) fun h =>
    Exists.casesOn h fun a ha => Or.inr (Exists.intro a (Eq.symm ha))

          DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.ScalarDef B I fun u => if u ∈ P then g u else h u
theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.scalarDef_ite : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {n : ℕ} {I : Set (Fin n → M)} (P : Set (Fin n → M)),
  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) P →
    ∀ {g h : (Fin n → M) → M},
      DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.ScalarDef B I g →
        DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.ScalarDef B I h →
          DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.ScalarDef B I fun u => if u ∈ P then g u else h u :=
fun {M} [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M]
    [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {n} {I} P hP {g h} hg hh =>
  have h1 := DiamondIntegral.Section7.BoundedClasses.graph_inter_definable I P (fun u => ![g u]) hg hP;
  have h2 := DiamondIntegral.Section7.ElementaryBlowups.graph_diff_definable I P (fun u => ![h u]) hh hP;
  id
    (Eq.mpr
      (eq_of_heq
        ((fun M sM hM B n sn A A' e'_7 =>
            Eq.casesOn (motive := fun a x =>
              A' = a →
                e'_7 ≍ x →
                  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A ≍
                    SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A')
              e'_7
              (fun h =>
                Eq.ndrec (motive := fun A' =>
                  ∀ (e_7 : A = A'),
                    e_7 ≍ Eq.refl A →
                      SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A ≍
                        SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A')
                  (fun e_7 h => HEq.refl (SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B sn A))
                  (Eq.symm h) e'_7)
              (Eq.refl A') (HEq.refl e'_7))
          M inst hM B (1 + n) (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv)
          (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn I fun u => ![(fun u => if u ∈ P then g u else h u) u])
          ((DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn (I ∩ P) fun u => ![g u]) ∪
            DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVMeasuredCharts.graphOn (I \ P) fun u => ![h u])
          (Set.ext fun w =>
            if hw : w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 n ∈ P then
              {
                mp := fun a =>
                  And.casesOn a fun hi he =>
                    Or.inl
                      ⟨⟨hi, hw⟩,
                        id
                          (Eq.mp
                            (congrFun'
                              (congrArg Eq
                                (congrFun'
                                  (congrArg Matrix.vecCons
                                    ((fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => (fun x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3 => if_pos hw) x_0 x_1 x_2 x_3)

                                        (w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 n ∈ P))
                                      M (g (w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 n))
                                      (h (w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 n))))
                                  ![]))
                              (w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 n))
                            he)⟩,
                mpr := fun a =>
                  Or.casesOn a
                    (fun h_1 =>
                      And.casesOn h_1 fun left he =>
                        And.casesOn left fun hi right =>
                          ⟨hi,
                            Eq.mpr

                                          (h (w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex 1 n))))
                                      ![]))
                                  (w ∘ DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex 1 n)))
                              he⟩)
                    fun h_1 => And.casesOn h_1 fun left right => And.casesOn left fun left hn => absurd hw hn }

theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.mem_coordEq : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {n : ℕ} {i : Fin n} {c : M} {u : Fin n → M},
  (u ∈
      SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.sortedUniverse fun x =>
        SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) →
    (u ∈ DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.coordEq i c ↔ u i = c) :=

    {c} {u} hu =>
  { mp := fun a => And.casesOn a fun he right => congrFun he 0,
    mpr := fun he =>
      ⟨funext fun j =>
          List.Mem.casesOn (motive := fun a x =>
            List.finRange (Nat.succ 0) = a → Fintype.complete j ≍ x → (u ∘ ![i]) j = ![c] j) (Fintype.complete j)
            (fun as h =>
              List.cons.noConfusion h fun head_eq =>
                Eq.ndrec (motive := fun j =>
                  ∀ (this : j ∈ Fintype.elems), [] ≍ as → this ≍ List.Mem.head as → (u ∘ ![i]) j = ![c] j)
                  (fun this tail_eq =>
                    Eq.ndrec (motive := fun as =>
                      this ≍ List.Mem.head as →
                        (u ∘ ![i]) ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl (Nat.succ 0)⟩) =
                          ![c] ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl (Nat.succ 0)⟩))
                      (fun h => he) (eq_of_heq tail_eq))
                  (eq_of_heq head_eq) (Fintype.complete j))
            (fun b {as} a h =>
              List.cons.noConfusion h fun head_eq =>
                Eq.ndrec (motive := fun b => [] ≍ as → Fintype.complete j ≍ List.Mem.tail b a → (u ∘ ![i]) j = ![c] j)
                  (fun tail_eq =>
                    Eq.ndrec (motive := fun {as} =>
                      ∀ (a : List.Mem j as),
                        Fintype.complete j ≍ List.Mem.tail ((fun i => i) ⟨0, Nat.le_refl (Nat.succ 0)⟩) a →
                          (u ∘ ![i]) j = ![c] j)
                      (fun a h =>
                        List.Mem.casesOn (motive := fun a_1 x => [] = a_1 → a ≍ x → (u ∘ ![i]) j = ![c] j) a
                          (fun as h => False.elim (noConfusion_of_Nat List.ctorIdx h))
                          (fun b {as} a_1 h => False.elim (noConfusion_of_Nat List.ctorIdx h)) (Eq.refl [])
                          (HEq.refl a))
                      (eq_of_heq tail_eq) a)
                  (eq_of_heq head_eq))
            (Eq.refl (List.finRange (Nat.succ 0))) (HEq.refl (Fintype.complete j)),
        hu⟩ }

theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.dec_enc : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF],
  M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF00 →
    ∀ (x : M),
      DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.dec (DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.yv x)
          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.tv x) =
        x :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] h00 x =>
  if hx : x = SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero then
    of_eq_true
      (Eq.trans
        (congr
          (congrArg Eq
            (ite_eq_left_of_eq_true SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero
              (DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.yv x)
              (Eq.trans
                (congrFun'
                  (congrArg Eq
                    (ite_eq_left_of_eq_true DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.minusOne
                      SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVOne
                      (Eq.trans (congrFun' (congrArg Eq hx) SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero)
                        (eq_self SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero))))
                  DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.minusOne)
                (eq_self DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.minusOne))))
          hx)
        (eq_self SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero))
  else
    have ht :=
      of_eq_true
        (Eq.trans
          (congrFun'
            (congrArg Eq
              (ite_eq_right_of_eq_false DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.minusOne
                SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVOne (eq_false hx)))
            SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVOne)
          (eq_self SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVOne));
    have hy :=
      of_eq_true
        (Eq.trans
          (congrFun' (congrArg Eq (ite_eq_right_of_eq_false SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVOne x (eq_false hx)))
            x)
          (eq_self x));
    Eq.mpr
      (id
        (congrArg (fun _a => _a = x)
          (DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.dec.eq_1 (DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.yv x)
            (DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.tv x))))
      (Eq.mpr
        (id
          (congrArg
            (fun _a =>
              (if _a = DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.minusOne then SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero
                else DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.yv x) =
                x)
            ht))
        (Eq.mpr
          (id
            (congrArg
              (fun _a =>
                (if SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVOne = DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.minusOne then
                    SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero
                  else _a) =
                  x)
              hy))
          (Eq.mpr
            (id
              (congrArg (fun _a => _a = x)
                (if_neg (Ne.symm (DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.minusOne_ne_one h00)))))
            (Eq.refl x))))

theorem DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.decode_tupleDef : ∀ {M : Type}
  [inst : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {B : SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.Base} {n : ℕ} {J : Set (Fin (n + n) → M)},
  SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase B
      (fun x => SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.SortTag.rv) J →
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.TupleDef B J DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.decode :=
fun {M} [SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF] {B} {n}
    {J} hJ =>
  DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.tupleDef_of_scalar hJ fun i =>
    DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.scalarDef_congr
      (DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.scalarDef_ite
        (DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.coordEq (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex n n i)
          DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.minusOne)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.coordEq_definable
          (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex n n i)
          DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.minusOne_definable)
        (DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.scalarDef_const hJ
          (DiamondIntegral.Section4.RVNamedTuples.zero_definable B))
        (DiamondIntegral.Section7.RVMaps.scalarDef_coord hJ
          (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex n n i)))
      fun z hz =>
      of_eq_true
        (Eq.trans
          (congrFun'
            (congrArg Eq
              (ite_congr
                ((fun x_0 x_1 =>
                    propext
                      ((fun x_0 x_1 =>
                          DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.mem_coordEq
                            (SpecialTransformations.Section3.GammaParameters.DefinableOverBase.proper hJ z hz))

                Eq.refl (z (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.headIndex n n i))))
            (if
                z (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex n n i) =
                  DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.minusOne then
              SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iRVZero

                z (DiamondIntegral.Section4.FiniteTupleBlocks.tailIndex n n i) =
                  DiamondIntegral.Section7.StrictCharts.minusOne then
